## Rules applied (mechanical)

1. Read tools must be backed by the helper tier (`resolve` / `findBy*` / `search` / `getContext`).
   The registry currently carries 62 helper-tier tools, 0 read
   tools are still backed by a plain primitive — see "Helper-tier gaps" for the worklist.
2. `listAll` / `listPages` are never projected (unbounded reads).
3. Every list tool states its bound; every helper scan states its cap.
4. Mutating tools expose a `dry_run` input affordance.
5. `sensitive` / `requiresApproval` are stated on the tool.
6. No binary/download tool (resources photos, public_photos, uploads, exports, s3_exports) is projected.
7. `MCP_TOOL_OVERRIDES.json` is the only place a description may change; this script never re-derives one.
8. A curation `exclude` override (`{tool, field: "exclude", newValue: true, reason}`) drops a projected
   tool: one tool per distinct outcome, so no two tools in the curated list do the same job.

Bounded: limit defaults to 25 with a hard maximum of 100, and the client scan stops at 500 records / 4 pages (it throws RESOLUTION_TRUNCATED rather than returning a partial answer). | hudu_get_website | description | "Resolve a website from an id, name or slug. Use when the identifier is a name or slug; websites.get is cheaper for a known id. Bounded: limit defaults to 25 with a hard maximum of 100, and the client scan stops at 500 records / 4 pages (it throws RESOLUTION_TRUNCATED rather than returning a partial answer). ## Excluded by curation (dropped through `MCP_TOOL_OVERRIDES.json`)

Dropped from the projection by Toolsmith curation, not by a rule: each one shared its
outcome (`backingOperation`) with a better-named tool that survives, or was otherwise
redundant. The reason column is the override's `reason`.

| hudu_websites_get | websites.get | read | primitive-read | Duplicate outcome: after re-pointing it backs onto websites.resolve, exactly like hudu_get_website (the retained name). hudu_get_website is the identifier form (id, name, slug, domain); the id-only alias adds nothing. A tools/list where two tools do the same job is worse than a slightly shorter list. |
| hudu_websites_list | websites.list | read | primitive-read | Duplicate outcome: after re-pointing it backs onto websites.search, exactly like hudu_search_websites (the retained name). hudu_search_websites is the search-first name for the same bounded read. A tools/list where two tools do the same job is worse than a slightly shorter list. |

Restore one by deleting its `exclude` override record and re-running `npm run mcp:project`.

### hudu_find_activity_logs_by_resource

- backingOperation: `activity_logs.findByResource`
- description: List the activity log entries for one resource. Preferred over activity_logs.listAll for 'what happened to this record'. Returns one record: a compact ActivityLogSummary (updated_at, … dropped); expand: true returns the full ActivityLog. Bounded: limit defaults to 25 with a hard maximum of 100, and the client scan stops at 500 records / 4 pages (it throws RESOLUTION_TRUNCATED rather than returning a partial answer). Do not use it to enumerate or to page: it returns at most one exact match (call hudu_search_activity_logs or hudu_get_activity_log for that).
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false
- annotations: effect="read", readOnlyHint=true, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=false, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, tier="helper", bounded="default 25 results, hard maximum 100; client scan capped at 500 records / 4 pages", helperTierBacked=true, helperTierBacking="activity_logs.findByResource"

- outputSchema: `{"type":"ActivityLogSummary","drops":["updated_at"],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"id","type":"number","required":false},{"name":"user_id","type":"number","required":false},{"name":"user_email","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"resource_id","type":"number","required":false},{"name":"resource_type","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"action_message","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"created_at","type":"string","required":false}],"fullType":"ActivityLog","expand":"expand: true returns the full typed record"}`

Do not use it for free-text discovery across many records: call hudu_search_matchers (or hudu_search_across_resources when the resource type is unknown).

### hudu_get_procedure_with_tasks

- backingOperation: `procedures.getWithTasks`
- description: Fetch a procedure together with its tasks in one call. Preferred over calling procedures.get and procedure_tasks.listAll yourself whenever the goal is to read or report on a process. Returns one record: a compact ProcedureWithTasks (limit, … dropped); expand: true returns the full ProcedureWithTasksFull. Bounded: every sub-list is fetched with limit, default 25, hard maximum 100 — no sub-fetch walks the account. Do not use it to page every procedure: it fetches one procedure plus its bounded task list.
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false
- annotations: effect="read", readOnlyHint=true, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=false, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, tier="helper", bounded="default 25 records per sub-list, hard maximum 100", helperTierBacked=true, helperTierBacking="procedures.getWithTasks"

### hudu_resolve_any

- backingOperation: `operations.resolveAny`
- description: Resolve an identifier against several resources and return the candidates that match. Preferred when the caller has an identifier but not the resource type; use the resource's own resolve when the type is known. Returns ResolutionCandidateHit rows for each resource that matched, with scanned/truncated counts; it does not throw RESOLUTION_AMBIGUOUS across resources. Bounded: limit defaults to 25 and is hard-capped at 100; a larger value throws CONFIG_ERROR and is never silently clamped. Do not use it when the resource type is known: call that resource's hudu_get_<singular>.

### hudu_search_across_resources

- backingOperation: `operations.searchAcrossResources`
- description: Search several Hudu resources for one query in a single bounded call. Preferred over calling several resources' search methods yourself whenever the caller does not know which resource holds the record. Returns one SearchHit row per match as { resource, id, label, item }, in resource order (expand: true returns the full typed records). Bounded: limit defaults to 25 and is hard-capped at 100; a larger value throws CONFIG_ERROR and is never silently clamped. Do not use it when the resource type is known: that resource's own hudu_search_<resource> issues one request instead of eight.
